<Callout type="insight" title="The framing that wins this final">
  Every section, every essay, every multiple choice question on the final test bends back to one structure: **Cause → Effect → Significance**. The teacher said it themselves at the top of the official study guide. Don't memorize dates as a list. Memorize them as **why this happened, what it caused, and why it matters for what comes next.**
</Callout>

<Callout type="tip" title="How to use this guide">
  1. **Start at the master overview** below: spin the Cesium globe to see all five sections at once, scrub the master timeline, scan the MindMap to feel the causal chain.
  2. **Review the master glossary** — every key term in one place.
  3. **Work through each of the five sections in order.** They build on each other: the unfinished business of WWI causes WWII; the Russian Revolution feeds straight into the Cold War.
  4. **Each section's "Check yourself" quiz** is your reality check. Take it, then cycle back to anything you missed.
  5. **End with the cumulative mock final** at the bottom: 50-minute timed test, mixed across all five sections, with re-rollable question banks.
</Callout>

## Master timeline, 1914 to 1991

77 years. ~50 events. Color-coded by section: WWI (red), Russian Revolution (crimson), Interwar (gray), WWII (red-orange), Cold War (light red).

<Timeline
  height="780px"
  title={{ headline: "Adv Modern Europe Final", text: "1914 to 1991: The 77 years that shaped the modern world" }}
  events={[
    { date: "1914-06-28", headline: "Franz Ferdinand assassinated in Sarajevo", text: "A 19-year-old Serbian nationalist named Gavrilo Princip fires two shots. Within 6 weeks, all of Europe is at war." },
    { date: "1914-07-28", headline: "Austria-Hungary declares war on Serbia", text: "Germany's 'blank check' encourages aggression. Russia mobilizes to defend Serbia." },
    { date: "1914-08-04", headline: "Britain enters the war", text: "Germany invades neutral Belgium. Britain honors its Belgium guarantee. The war is now European." },
    { date: "1914-09-05", headline: "First Battle of the Marne", text: "Schlieffen Plan fails. Western Front becomes a stalemate. Trench warfare begins." },
    { date: "1915-04-25", headline: "Gallipoli campaign begins", text: "British/ANZAC attempt to seize Constantinople. Disastrous failure." },
    { date: "1915-05-23", headline: "Italy joins the Allies", text: "Italy switches from the Triple Alliance after Britain promises Italian-speaking lands." },
    { date: "1916-02-21", headline: "Battle of Verdun begins", text: "Germany attacks France's fortress. 10 months. 700,000 casualties. No territory changes hands." },
    { date: "1916-07-01", headline: "Battle of the Somme begins", text: "British offensive. 60,000 British casualties on day one. 1+ million total." },
    { date: "1917-04-06", headline: "United States declares war on Germany", text: "Unrestricted submarine warfare + Zimmerman Note + economic ties pull the US in." },
    { date: "1917-03-08", headline: "March Revolution in Russia", text: "Women textile workers strike on International Women's Day. Within days, Tsar Nicholas II abdicates." },
    { date: "1917-11-07", headline: "Bolshevik Revolution", text: "Lenin's Bolsheviks seize power. 'Peace, Land, Bread.'" },
    { date: "1918-03-03", headline: "Treaty of Brest-Litovsk", text: "Russia exits WWI. Loses huge territory. Germany frees up Eastern Front troops." },
    { date: "1918-07-15", headline: "Second Battle of the Marne", text: "Last German offensive. 2 million fresh US troops break it. Germany retreats." },
    { date: "1918-11-11", headline: "Armistice", text: "Germany signs ceasefire. 11th hour, 11th day, 11th month. WWI ends." },
    { date: "1919-06-28", headline: "Treaty of Versailles signed", text: "Article 231 'War Guilt Clause' blames Germany. Sets up resentment that fuels WWII." },
    { date: "1919-08-11", headline: "Weimar Republic founded", text: "New German democracy. Saddled with reparations and the stigma of signing Versailles." },
    { date: "1921-03-15", headline: "Lenin announces NEP", text: "New Economic Policy. Tactical retreat from full communism. Peasants can sell extra crops." },
    { date: "1922-10-28", headline: "Mussolini's March on Rome", text: "Black Shirts march. King appoints Mussolini PM. First fascist government." },
    { date: "1922-12-30", headline: "USSR formed", text: "Russia, Ukraine, Belarus, Transcaucasia merge into the Union of Soviet Socialist Republics." },
    { date: "1923-11-08", headline: "Beer Hall Putsch", text: "Hitler's failed Munich coup. Imprisoned. Writes Mein Kampf in prison." },
    { date: "1923-11-15", headline: "German hyperinflation peaks", text: "200 billion marks for one loaf of bread. Wheelbarrows of cash to buy groceries." },
    { date: "1924-08-30", headline: "Dawes Plan", text: "200 million dollar US loan restructures German reparations. Stabilizes Europe briefly." },
    { date: "1924-01-21", headline: "Lenin dies", text: "Succession battle. Stalin outmaneuvers Trotsky." },
    { date: "1928-10-01", headline: "First Five-Year Plan begins", text: "Stalin's forced industrialization. Rapid growth, massive human cost." },
    { date: "1929-10-29", headline: "Black Tuesday: Wall Street crashes", text: "US stock market collapse triggers Great Depression. Spreads globally." },
    { date: "1931-09-18", headline: "Japan invades Manchuria", text: "League of Nations fails its first test. Japan walks out of the League." },
    { date: "1933-01-30", headline: "Hitler becomes Chancellor", text: "Appointed legally. Reichstag Fire (Feb 27) leads to Enabling Act. Democracy ends." },
    { date: "1935-09-15", headline: "Nuremberg Laws", text: "Strip German Jews of citizenship. Prelude to the Holocaust." },
    { date: "1936-03-07", headline: "Hitler remilitarizes the Rhineland", text: "Violates Versailles. France and Britain do nothing. Appeasement begins." },
    { date: "1936-07-17", headline: "Spanish Civil War begins", text: "Franco's nationalists, backed by Germany and Italy, fight the Republic. Test run for WWII." },
    { date: "1937-12-13", headline: "Rape of Nanjing", text: "Japanese army massacres 200,000+ Chinese civilians over 6 weeks." },
    { date: "1938-09-30", headline: "Munich Agreement", text: "Britain + France hand Hitler the Sudetenland. Chamberlain: 'Peace for our time.'" },
    { date: "1939-08-23", headline: "Nazi-Soviet Nonaggression Pact", text: "Hitler and Stalin secretly divide Poland. The world is stunned." },
    { date: "1939-09-01", headline: "Germany invades Poland", text: "Blitzkrieg in 3 weeks. Britain and France declare war. WWII begins." },
    { date: "1941-06-22", headline: "Operation Barbarossa", text: "Hitler breaks the pact and invades the USSR. Largest invasion in history." },
    { date: "1941-12-07", headline: "Pearl Harbor", text: "Japan attacks. US enters WWII." },
    { date: "1942-08-23", headline: "Battle of Stalingrad begins", text: "USSR defeats Germany over 5 months. 2 million casualties. Eastern Front turning point." },
    { date: "1942-06-04", headline: "Battle of Midway", text: "US Navy destroys 4 Japanese carriers. Pacific turning point." },
    { date: "1944-06-06", headline: "D-Day", text: "Largest amphibious invasion in history. Second front opens in Western Europe." },
    { date: "1945-02-04", headline: "Yalta Conference", text: "Roosevelt, Churchill, Stalin. Germany to be divided. Free-elections promise (broken)." },
    { date: "1945-05-08", headline: "V-E Day", text: "Germany surrenders. War in Europe ends." },
    { date: "1945-07-17", headline: "Potsdam Conference", text: "Truman replaces FDR. Demands free elections. Stalin refuses. Cold War begins." },
    { date: "1945-08-06", headline: "Hiroshima atomic bomb", text: "First atomic weapon used in war. ~140,000 dead." },
    { date: "1945-08-09", headline: "Nagasaki atomic bomb", text: "Japan surrenders Aug 14. WWII ends." },
    { date: "1947-03-12", headline: "Truman Doctrine", text: "US aid to countries resisting communism. Containment policy formalized." },
    { date: "1948-06-24", headline: "Berlin Airlift begins", text: "Stalin blocks West Berlin. US/UK fly in supplies for 11 months." },
    { date: "1949-04-04", headline: "NATO formed", text: "12 Western nations. Mutual defense alliance." },
    { date: "1955-05-14", headline: "Warsaw Pact formed", text: "USSR + 7 satellites. Direct response to NATO." },
    { date: "1957-10-04", headline: "Sputnik launched", text: "First artificial satellite. Space Race begins." },
    { date: "1961-08-13", headline: "Berlin Wall built", text: "East Germany seals off West Berlin. The wall stands 28 years." },
    { date: "1962-10-16", headline: "Cuban Missile Crisis", text: "13 days. Closest the world ever came to nuclear war." },
    { date: "1985-03-11", headline: "Gorbachev takes power", text: "Glasnost, perestroika, democratization. Begins to reform from within." },
    { date: "1989-11-09", headline: "Berlin Wall falls", text: "East Germans cross freely. Communism collapses across Eastern Europe." },
    { date: "1991-12-25", headline: "USSR dissolves", text: "Gorbachev resigns. 15 republics independent. Cold War over." }
  ]}
/>

## The causal chain at a glance

This MindMap shows the spine of the entire 77 years. Every section's outcome becomes the next section's setup.

<MindMap
  title="How the five sections connect"
  height={520}
  markdown={`# 1914 to 1991
## 1. WWI
- MAIN causes (Militarism, Alliances, Imperialism, Nationalism)
- Trench warfare = stalemate
- Russia exits early (Brest-Litovsk)
- US enters late (Zimmerman Note, U-boats)
- Treaty of Versailles -> German resentment
## 2. Russian Revolution
- Tsar Nicholas II falls (March 1917)
- Provisional Government fails
- Lenin's Bolsheviks (November 1917)
- NEP buys time
- Stalin's totalitarianism (Five-Year Plans, Great Purge)
## 3. Interwar
- Weimar weakness + hyperinflation
- Great Depression (1929) globalizes the crisis
- Mussolini + Hitler rise
- Appeasement (Munich 1938) fails
- Nazi-Soviet Pact 1939 clears the path
## 4. WWII
- Blitzkrieg conquers Europe
- Stalingrad + Midway + D-Day = turning points
- Holocaust
- Atomic bombs end the war in the Pacific
- US + USSR emerge as superpowers -> Cold War setup
## 5. Cold War
- Yalta + Potsdam break the alliance
- Containment + Truman Doctrine + Marshall Plan + NATO
- Cuban Missile Crisis = brinkmanship peak
- Detente then renewed tension
- Gorbachev's reforms collapse the USSR`}
/>

## Master glossary

Every key term from the official Final Exam Study Guide and chapter-specific key-term sheets. Hover any term in any section's prose to see this definition.

### World War I (Section 1)

<div class="not-prose my-6 rounded-2xl border border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800 overflow-hidden">
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Triple Alliance</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1882 alliance: Germany, Austria-Hungary, Italy. (Italy switches sides in 1915.)</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Triple Entente</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1907 alliance: France, Russia, Great Britain.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Militarism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Glorifying the military and keeping a large, ready-to-fight army. Pre-1914 Europe was in an arms race.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Alliance System</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Network of secret defense agreements. When one country fell into war, the rest were dragged in by treaty.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Imperialism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Stronger nations controlling weaker ones for resources, markets, and prestige. A pre-1914 source of friction.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Nationalism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Pride and loyalty to one's nation or ethnic group. United Germans and Italians; tore apart Austria-Hungary and Ottoman Empire.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Balkan Peninsula (powder keg)</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Southeastern Europe. Many ethnic groups, many revolts, multiple empires colliding. Where WWI started.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">July Crisis</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">The 5 weeks between Franz Ferdinand's assassination (June 28, 1914) and Britain entering the war (Aug 4, 1914). Cascading diplomatic failure.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Archduke Franz Ferdinand</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Heir to the Austro-Hungarian throne. Assassinated June 28, 1914 in Sarajevo by Gavrilo Princip.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Black Hand</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Serbian nationalist secret society that backed Princip. The connection that triggered Austria-Hungary's ultimatum to Serbia.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Blank Check</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Germany's promise of unconditional support to Austria-Hungary in July 1914. Encouraged A-H to attack Serbia.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Central Powers</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Germany, Austria-Hungary, Ottoman Empire, Bulgaria.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Allied Powers</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Britain, France, Russia (until 1917), Italy (from 1915), United States (from 1917), Japan, plus colonial troops.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Western Front</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">The trench line from the English Channel to Switzerland. Stalemate for 4 years.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Schlieffen Plan</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Germany's pre-war plan to defeat France quickly through Belgium, then turn east to fight Russia. Failed at the First Battle of the Marne.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">First Battle of the Marne</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">September 1914. France stops the German advance. Schlieffen Plan dies. Stalemate begins.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Battle of Verdun</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">February to December 1916. German attack on French fortress. ~700,000 casualties. Symbol of the futility of WWI.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Battle of the Somme</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">July to November 1916. British offensive. Over 1 million casualties. Symbol of industrial-scale warfare.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Trench Warfare</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Soldiers dug into long defensive ditches. Machine guns, barbed wire, and artillery made attacks suicidal. Caused the stalemate.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Stalemate</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">A standstill where neither side can win. Defined the Western Front for nearly 4 years.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">US Isolationism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">American policy of staying out of European conflicts. Held until Germany's submarine warfare and the Zimmerman Note made neutrality impossible.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Unrestricted Submarine Warfare</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Germany sank any ship in war zones, including civilian. The sinking of the Lusitania (1915) and resumption of the policy in 1917 pulled the US toward war.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Zimmerman Note</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">January 1917. Germany secretly proposed an alliance with Mexico against the US. British intercepted and shared with the US. Tipped public opinion to war.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Total War</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Entire society mobilized for the war effort. Civilian factories make weapons. Rationing. Propaganda. Women in the workforce. Every resource directed at victory.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Propaganda</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Government messaging to shape public opinion. Posters, films, news. Demonized the enemy ("the Hun"), recruited soldiers, sold war bonds.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Eastern Front</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Russia vs Germany and Austria-Hungary. Less trench-bound than the West. Russia bore enormous casualties (4+ million in year one).</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Gallipoli Campaign</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1915 Allied attempt to seize the Dardanelles and Constantinople. Failed disastrously. ~250,000 Allied casualties.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Treaty of Brest-Litovsk</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">March 1918. Russia exits the war. Loses Poland, Ukraine, Baltics, Finland. Frees up German troops for the Western Front.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Second Battle of the Marne</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">July 1918. Germany's last offensive. 2 million fresh US troops break it. Germany begins retreating.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Armistice</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Ceasefire. November 11, 1918, 11 a.m. Germany signs. WWI ends.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Paris Peace Conference</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">January 1919. Big Four (US, UK, France, Italy) negotiate the postwar order. No Germany, no Russia.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Woodrow Wilson</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">US president. Brought idealism (Fourteen Points) to a Europe that wanted revenge.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Fourteen Points</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Wilson's plan: end secret alliances, freedom of seas, reduce militaries, fair colonial claims, self-determination, League of Nations.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">League of Nations</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">First international body to prevent war. Wilson's idea. The US Senate refused to join. Doomed it from the start.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Treaty of Versailles</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">June 28, 1919. Punished Germany: lost territory, lost colonies, capped army at 100,000, paid reparations. Sowed the seeds of WWII.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Article 231 (War Guilt Clause)</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Forced Germany to accept full blame for WWI. Justification for reparations. Bred the resentment that Hitler exploited.</span></div>
</div>

### Russian Revolution (Section 2)

<div class="not-prose my-6 rounded-2xl border border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800 overflow-hidden">
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Provisional Government</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Temporary government after the March 1917 abdication of Tsar Nicholas II. Failed because it kept Russia in WWI and didn't redistribute land.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Bolsheviks</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Lenin's radical communist faction. Seized power in November 1917. "Peace, Land, Bread."</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Vladimir Lenin</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Leader of the Bolshevik Revolution. Returned from exile via German train. First Soviet leader. Died 1924.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Soviets</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Worker and soldier councils. Parallel power to the Provisional Government. Lenin's slogan: "All power to the Soviets."</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Russian Civil War</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1918-1921. Reds (Bolsheviks) vs Whites (anti-Bolsheviks, plus foreign interventionists). Reds win.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Leon Trotsky</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Lenin's chief lieutenant. Built and led the Red Army during the Civil War. Lost the succession battle to Stalin. Exiled, later assassinated.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">NEP (New Economic Policy)</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Lenin's 1921 retreat from full communism. Peasants could sell extra crops. Small businesses allowed. Tactical, not ideological.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">USSR</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Union of Soviet Socialist Republics. Formed 1922. Russia plus Ukraine, Belarus, Transcaucasia. Eventually 15 republics.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Joseph Stalin</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Lenin's successor (1924-1953). Five-Year Plans, collectivization, Great Purge. Killed millions. Built the USSR into a superpower.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Totalitarianism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Government that controls every aspect of life: economy, religion, education, media, family. Stalin's USSR is the textbook case.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Five-Year Plans</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Stalin's forced industrialization. Set production targets across the economy. Rapid growth. Massive human cost.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Collectivization</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Forced merger of peasant farms into giant state-run collective farms. Kulaks resisted, were liquidated. Famine killed millions.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Great Purge</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1936-1938. Stalin's mass arrests and executions. ~8 to 13 million dead. Eliminated rivals, generals, Old Bolsheviks. Show trials.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Cult of Personality</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Propaganda treats the leader as godlike and infallible. Stalin's portraits everywhere. History rewritten to center him.</span></div>
</div>

### Interwar Years (Section 3)

<div class="not-prose my-6 rounded-2xl border border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800 overflow-hidden">
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Weimar Republic</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">German democracy, 1919-1933. Saddled with Versailles guilt, hyperinflation, depression. Collapsed when Hitler came to power.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Article 48</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Weimar emergency-powers clause. Hitler used it after the Reichstag Fire to suspend civil liberties.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Proportional Representation</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Weimar's voting system. Tiny parties got seats. Coalitions were unstable. Made governing nearly impossible.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Hyperinflation</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Germany 1923. 200 billion marks for a loaf of bread. Wheelbarrows of cash. Wiped out middle-class savings. Bred resentment.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Dawes Plan</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1924. US loaned 200 million dollars to restructure German reparations. Briefly stabilized Europe. Made Germany dependent on US loans.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Stock Market Crash (1929)</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">October 29, 1929 ("Black Tuesday"). US stock market collapses. Triggers the Great Depression. Spreads globally through American loans.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Great Depression</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1929-1939. Global economic collapse. Unemployment hit 30 percent. Trade dropped 62 percent. Discredited democracy in many countries.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Tariffs</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Taxes on imports. Countries raised them to protect domestic industries. Choked global trade. Made the Depression worse.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Collective Security</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Idea behind the League of Nations. If one nation is attacked, others respond together. Failed in the 1930s when nobody enforced it.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Fascism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Authoritarian, nationalist, militaristic ideology. Worships the state and the leader. Glorifies violence. Italy under Mussolini, Germany under Hitler.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Benito Mussolini</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Italian dictator. Founded fascism. March on Rome 1922. Modeled the playbook Hitler followed.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Adolf Hitler</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">German dictator. Nazi Party leader. Chancellor 1933. Caused WWII and the Holocaust. Suicide April 30, 1945.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Nazism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">German fascism. Adds racial ideology: Aryan supremacy, anti-Semitism, Lebensraum (living space).</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Anti-Semitism</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Hatred of Jews. Core to Nazi ideology. Led to Nuremberg Laws (1935), Kristallnacht (1938), and the Holocaust.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Lebensraum</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">"Living space." Hitler's claim that Germany needed Eastern European land to grow. Justification for invading Poland and the USSR.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Führer Principle</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Absolute obedience to the leader. The Führer is the state. No checks, no balances, no dissent.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Beer Hall Putsch</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">November 1923. Hitler's failed Munich coup. Imprisoned. Wrote Mein Kampf in prison.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Mein Kampf</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Hitler's 1925 memoir/manifesto. Spelled out his goals: anti-Semitism, Lebensraum, undoing Versailles. Few took it seriously until too late.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Remilitarization of the Rhineland</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">March 1936. Hitler moves troops into the buffer zone. Violates Versailles. France and Britain do nothing. Appeasement begins.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Invasion of Manchuria</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">September 1931. Japan seizes Manchuria from China. League of Nations condemns but does nothing. Japan walks out of the League.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Rape of Nanjing</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">December 1937 - January 1938. Japanese army massacres ~200,000 Chinese civilians and POWs. War crime defining the Pacific theater.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Munich Agreement</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">September 1938. Britain, France, Germany, Italy meet. Hand Hitler the Sudetenland. Czechoslovakia not invited. Chamberlain: "Peace for our time."</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Appeasement</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Giving in to an aggressor's demands to avoid war. Britain and France did this with Hitler 1936-1938. It encouraged him.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Axis Powers</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Germany, Italy, Japan. Anti-Comintern Pact 1936. Tripartite Pact 1940.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Neutrality Acts</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">US laws of the 1930s designed to keep America out of foreign wars. Banned arms sales to belligerents. Loosened as WWII approached.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Nazi-Soviet Nonaggression Pact</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">August 1939. Hitler and Stalin secretly agree not to attack each other and divide Poland. Stunned the world. Cleared the path to WWII.</span></div>
</div>

### World War II (Section 4)

<div class="not-prose my-6 rounded-2xl border border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800 overflow-hidden">
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Blitzkrieg</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">"Lightning war." Fast tank columns + dive bombers + radio coordination. Conquered Poland in 3 weeks, France in 6.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Battle of Britain</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Summer-Fall 1940. RAF defeats Luftwaffe. Hitler postpones invasion of Britain. First German setback.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Operation Barbarossa</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">June 22, 1941. Hitler's invasion of the USSR. Largest invasion in history. Initial success, then bogged down.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Pearl Harbor</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">December 7, 1941. Japan's surprise attack on US Pacific Fleet. Pulls the US into WWII.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Battle of Stalingrad</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">August 1942 - February 1943. USSR defeats German Sixth Army. ~2 million casualties. Eastern Front turning point.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Battle of Midway</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">June 1942. US Navy destroys 4 Japanese carriers. Pacific theater turning point.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">D-Day</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">June 6, 1944. Largest amphibious invasion in history. Allies land at Normandy. Opens the second front. ~3,000 Allied dead on day one.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Holocaust</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Systematic Nazi murder of 6 million Jews and 5 million others (Roma, disabled, Soviet POWs, political prisoners). Death camps: Auschwitz, Treblinka, Sobibor, etc.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Atomic Bomb</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Nuclear weapon. US dropped on Hiroshima (Aug 6, 1945) and Nagasaki (Aug 9, 1945). Japan surrendered Aug 14.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">V-E Day</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">May 8, 1945. Victory in Europe. Germany surrenders.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">V-J Day</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">August 14-15, 1945. Victory over Japan. Japan surrenders after the atomic bombs.</span></div>
</div>

### Cold War (Section 5)

<div class="not-prose my-6 rounded-2xl border border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800 overflow-hidden">
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Cold War</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1945-1991 political and ideological conflict between the US and USSR. Tension, competition, threats. No direct fighting between the two.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Yalta Conference</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">February 1945. FDR + Churchill + Stalin in Crimea. Germany to be divided. Free-elections promise (Stalin breaks it).</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Potsdam Conference</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">July 1945. Truman + Attlee + Stalin near Berlin. Truman demands free elections. Stalin refuses. Alliance breaks.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Iron Curtain</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Churchill's 1946 phrase. The line dividing democratic Western Europe from Soviet-controlled Eastern Europe.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Containment</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">US master strategy. Stop the spread of communism without direct war. Drove every Cold War policy.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Truman Doctrine</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1947. US aid to countries resisting communism. First applied to Greece and Turkey.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Marshall Plan</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1947. 12.5 billion dollars to rebuild Western Europe. Strengthened economies and undercut communism's appeal.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Berlin Airlift</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1948-1949. Stalin blocks West Berlin. US/UK fly in 2.3 million tons of supplies over 11 months. Stalin lifts the blockade.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">NATO</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">North Atlantic Treaty Organization. 1949. US, Canada, Western Europe. Mutual defense alliance.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Warsaw Pact</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">1955. USSR + 7 Eastern European satellites. Direct response to NATO and West Germany joining NATO.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Brinkmanship</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Pushing a conflict to the very edge of war so the other side backs down. Eisenhower-era strategy.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Sputnik</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">First artificial satellite. USSR launched it October 4, 1957. Sparked the Space Race and reshaped US science education.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Cuban Missile Crisis</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">October 1962. 13 days. USSR places nuclear missiles in Cuba. JFK quarantines. Closest the world came to nuclear war.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">John F. Kennedy</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">US president during the Cuban Missile Crisis. His handling defined nuclear-age diplomacy.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Fidel Castro</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Cuban revolutionary. Overthrew Batista in 1959. Allied Cuba with the USSR. The 90-mile-from-Florida communist state.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Détente</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">French for "easing of tension." Nixon's 1970s policy: relax Cold War hostilities through diplomacy. Opens China, signs SALT I.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Mikhail Gorbachev</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Last Soviet leader (1985-1991). Glasnost, perestroika, democratization. His reforms unintentionally collapsed the USSR.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Glasnost</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Russian for "openness." Free speech, free press, political transparency. Gorbachev's reform.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3 border-b border-ink-200 dark:border-ink-800"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Perestroika</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Russian for "restructuring." Limited free markets, allowed small businesses. Gorbachev's economic reform.</span></div>
  <div class="grid sm:grid-cols-[200px_1fr] gap-x-6 px-5 py-3"><strong class="font-serif font-semibold text-ink-900 dark:text-ink-100">Berlin Wall</strong><span class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Built August 13, 1961, by East Germany. Stopped people fleeing to the West. Fell November 9, 1989. Symbol of the Cold War.</span></div>
</div>

---

# Section 1 · World War I

<div class="not-prose relative my-6 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden" style="min-height: 220px;">
  <Particles preset="links" backgroundColor="#1a0606" particleColor="#7f1d1d" height={220} density={28} />
  <div class="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center px-6 pointer-events-none">
    <p class="font-mono text-white/60 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.25em] mb-3">Chapter 13 · 1914 to 1919</p>
    <h2 class="font-serif text-3xl md:text-5xl font-bold text-white mb-2">The Great War</h2>
    <p class="text-white/80 max-w-xl italic">"It will be over by Christmas." It wasn't.</p>
  </div>
</div>

<div class="not-prose my-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
  <TremorCard label="Duration" value="4 years 3 months" helper="July 28, 1914 to November 11, 1918" />
  <TremorCard label="Total casualties" value="~40 million" helper="20 million dead, 21 million wounded across all powers" />
</div>

<Callout type="insight" title="Why this section matters">
  WWI is where the modern world starts. Empires fall, communism takes power in Russia, the US enters Europe for the first time, and the bad peace at Versailles plants every seed of WWII. Master the **MAIN** causes, the trench-warfare stalemate, why the US entered late, and why the Treaty of Versailles created instability instead of stability.
</Callout>

## Section timeline

## Cause

Europe had been mostly peaceful for 30 years. By 1914, four pressures had built underneath that peace until any spark would set it off.

### The MAIN causes

<div class="not-prose my-6 grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
  <div class="rounded-xl border-2 border-accent-700 bg-accent-50 dark:bg-accent-950/30 p-4">
    <p class="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-accent-700 dark:text-accent-400 mb-2">M</p>
    <h4 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-accent-900 dark:text-accent-100 mb-2">Militarism</h4>
    <p class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Glorifying the military and racing to build the strongest army. Germany and Britain ran a naval arms race for two decades.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border-2 border-accent-700 bg-accent-50 dark:bg-accent-950/30 p-4">
    <p class="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-accent-700 dark:text-accent-400 mb-2">A</p>
    <h4 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-accent-900 dark:text-accent-100 mb-2">Alliances</h4>
    <p class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Secret defense agreements. When one country fell into war, every signatory got dragged in by treaty obligation.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border-2 border-accent-700 bg-accent-50 dark:bg-accent-950/30 p-4">
    <p class="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-accent-700 dark:text-accent-400 mb-2">I</p>
    <h4 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-accent-900 dark:text-accent-100 mb-2">Imperialism</h4>
    <p class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Stronger nations carving up the rest of the world. By 1914 most of Africa and Asia was European-owned. Friction over colonies.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border-2 border-accent-700 bg-accent-50 dark:bg-accent-950/30 p-4">
    <p class="font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-accent-700 dark:text-accent-400 mb-2">N</p>
    <h4 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-accent-900 dark:text-accent-100 mb-2">Nationalism</h4>
    <p class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Pride in one's nation or ethnic group. Unified Germans and Italians. Tore Austria-Hungary and the Ottomans apart from inside.</p>
  </div>
</div>

<Callout type="tip" title="Memory hook: MAIN">
  **M**ilitarism · **A**lliances · **I**mperialism · **N**ationalism. Memorize this acronym. It's the backbone of any WWI essay answer.
</Callout>

How those four causes plus the assassination trigger combined to start a world war:

```mermaid
flowchart LR
  M[Militarism<br/>European arms race] --> WAR[WWI<br/>begins August 1914]
  A[Alliances<br/>Triple Entente vs Triple Alliance] --> WAR
  I[Imperialism<br/>colonial rivalries] --> WAR
  N[Nationalism<br/>Balkans tensions] --> WAR
  T[Trigger:<br/>Franz Ferdinand assassinated<br/>June 28, 1914] --> WAR
  WAR --> S[Within 6 weeks:<br/>all great powers at war]
```

### The two pre-war alliances

<div class="not-prose my-6 grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
  <div class="rounded-2xl border-2 border-blue-700 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/30 p-5">
    <p class="font-mono uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[11px] font-bold text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300 mb-2">1882</p>
    <h3 class="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900 dark:text-blue-100 mb-3">Triple Alliance</h3>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"><Flag code="DE" size={28} /><span>Germany</span></div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"><Flag code="AT" size={28} /><span>Austria-Hungary</span></div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"><Flag code="IT" size={28} /><span>Italy <span class="text-xs text-ink-500">(switches sides 1915)</span></span></div>
  </div>
  <div class="rounded-2xl border-2 border-accent-700 bg-accent-50 dark:bg-accent-950/30 p-5">
    <p class="font-mono uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[11px] font-bold text-accent-800 dark:text-accent-300 mb-2">1907</p>
    <h3 class="font-serif text-2xl font-bold text-accent-900 dark:text-accent-100 mb-3">Triple Entente</h3>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"><Flag code="FR" size={28} /><span>France</span></div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"><Flag code="RU" size={28} /><span>Russia</span></div>
    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2"><Flag code="GB" size={28} /><span>Great Britain</span></div>
  </div>
</div>

### The Balkan powder keg

The Balkan Peninsula had everything needed for an explosion: many ethnic groups, a long history of revolts, multiple empires colliding (Austrian, Ottoman, Russian), and newly independent countries trying to expand.

<MapLibreView
  center={[20, 43]}
  zoom={5}
  height={360}
  style="https://demotiles.maplibre.org/style.json"
  markers={[
    { lng: 18.4131, lat: 43.8563, name: "Sarajevo", description: "June 28, 1914. Franz Ferdinand assassinated here. Trigger of WWI." },
    { lng: 20.4633, lat: 44.8176, name: "Belgrade", description: "Capital of Serbia. Slavic nationalist leadership." },
    { lng: 16.3738, lat: 48.2082, name: "Vienna", description: "Capital of Austria-Hungary. The empire that was insulted." },
    { lng: 28.9784, lat: 41.0082, name: "Constantinople", description: "Capital of the dying Ottoman Empire." },
    { lng: 23.7275, lat: 37.9838, name: "Athens", description: "Greece, eventually Allied." }
  ]}
/>

In **1908**, Austria-Hungary annexed Bosnia and Herzegovina, infuriating Serbia, which wanted to rule the Slavic populations there. The fuse was lit. It only needed a match.

### The July Crisis (1914): step by step

<StepThrough
  title="From assassination to world war in 5 weeks"
  steps={[
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:48px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">1</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">June 28, 1914 · Sarajevo</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Archduke <strong>Franz Ferdinand</strong>, heir to Austria-Hungary, is assassinated by <strong>Gavrilo Princip</strong>, a 19-year-old Serbian nationalist tied to the <strong>Black Hand</strong>.</p></div>`,
      caption: "The spark."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:48px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">2</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">Germany's "Blank Check"</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Germany promises Austria-Hungary unconditional support against Serbia. This emboldens A-H to demand the impossible.</p></div>`,
      caption: "The escalation."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:48px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">3</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">July 28 · A-H declares war on Serbia</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Serbia accepts most of A-H's harsh ultimatum, but not all. Austria-Hungary declares war anyway. Russia begins mobilizing to defend Serbia.</p></div>`,
      caption: "The first declaration."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:48px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">4</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">Aug 1-3 · Germany declares war on Russia and France</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">The alliance system kicks in like dominoes. Germany invades neutral Belgium en route to France.</p></div>`,
      caption: "The cascade."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:48px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">5</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">Aug 4 · Britain enters</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Britain had a treaty obligation to defend Belgian neutrality. The local Serbia conflict is now a European war. Within months, Japan and the Ottomans join. The world is at war.</p></div>`,
      caption: "Six weeks. Five great powers. One world war."
    }
  ]}
  height={300}
/>

<HistoryAnimation
  title="The Domino Falls"
  scenes={[
    {
      duration: 4,
      caption: "June 28, 1914. A 19-year-old steps out of a Sarajevo café.",
      svg: `<svg viewBox="0 0 400 240" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
        <rect width="400" height="240" fill="#fff"/>
        <text x="200" y="40" text-anchor="middle" font-family="serif" font-size="14" fill="#52525b">SARAJEVO</text>
        <g stroke="#000" stroke-width="2" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round">
          <circle cx="200" cy="120" r="10"/>
          <line x1="200" y1="130" x2="200" y2="170"/>
          <line x1="200" y1="145" x2="180" y2="155"/>
          <line x1="200" y1="145" x2="220" y2="155"/>
          <line x1="200" y1="170" x2="190" y2="200"/>
          <line x1="200" y1="170" x2="210" y2="200"/>
        </g>
        <text x="200" y="225" text-anchor="middle" font-family="serif" font-size="12" fill="#000">Princip · 19 · tuberculosis · two pistol shots</text>
      </svg>`
    },
    {

      svg: `<svg viewBox="0 0 400 240" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">

### The two sides during the war

    </ul>
  </div>

## Effect

### The Schlieffen Plan and how it failed

Germany feared a **two-front war**: France in the west, Russia in the east. The **Schlieffen Plan** said: smash France quickly through Belgium, then turn east before Russia could finish mobilizing.

It depended on speed. It got neither.

<StepThrough
  title="Why the Schlieffen Plan failed"
  steps={[
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:14px;">1. Belgium fights back</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Belgium was supposed to be a quick highway to France. Instead, the Belgian army held up the German advance for weeks. The clock was already broken.</p></div>`,
      caption: "Plan: speed. Reality: resistance."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:14px;">2. Russia mobilizes faster than predicted</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Germany expected Russia to take 6 weeks. It took 10 days. Germany had to peel off troops to defend the Eastern Front, weakening the western drive.</p></div>`,
      caption: "Plan: one front at a time. Reality: two fronts immediately."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:14px;">3. First Battle of the Marne (Sept 1914)</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">German forces were 30 miles from Paris. The Allies regrouped along the Marne River and pushed them back. Schlieffen Plan dies. Quick war becomes long war.</p></div>`,
      caption: "Stalemate begins. Trenches start being dug."
    }
  ]}
  height={300}
/>

### Trench warfare and the Western Front stalemate

<div class="not-prose my-6 rounded-xl border border-ink-300 dark:border-ink-700 bg-ink-50 dark:bg-ink-950 p-6">
  <p class="text-sm text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300 mb-4 font-mono uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-[11px] font-semibold">A typical trench cross-section</p>
  <pre class="text-xs leading-tight font-mono text-ink-800 dark:text-ink-200 overflow-x-auto">
   own side                 NO MAN'S LAND                    enemy side
   ────────────             ────────────────────             ────────────
   ║ trench  ║ → barbed wire → craters/mud/corpses → barbed wire ← ║ trench  ║
   ║ sandbag ║                                                     ║ sandbag ║
   ║ duck-   ║   machine guns sweep at any movement                ║ duck-   ║
   ║ board   ║   poison gas drifts on the wind                     ║ board   ║
   ║ dug-out ║   artillery shells obliterate fortifications        ║ dug-out ║
  </pre>
</div>

By early 1915, both sides had dug 400+ miles of trenches stretching from the English Channel to Switzerland. The land between, **No Man's Land**, became a wasteland of barbed wire, mud, and craters.

<div class="not-prose my-6 grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
  <TremorCard label="Western Front length" value="~440 miles" helper="From the English Channel to the Swiss border" />
  <TremorCard label="Trench depth" value="6-8 feet" helper="Deep enough to stand and walk hidden from artillery" />
  <TremorCard label="Conditions" value="Rats, mud, lice" helper="Standing water in waist-deep trenches caused 'trench foot' that rotted toes" />
  <TremorCard label="Stalemate duration" value="~4 years" helper="The lines barely moved between 1914 and 1918" />
</div>

### Modern warfare = mass casualties

Industrial-era weapons collided with 19th-century tactics. The result was massacre.

<div class="not-prose my-6 grid sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
  <div class="rounded-xl border border-ink-300 dark:border-ink-700 p-4 bg-white dark:bg-ink-900"><h4 class="font-serif font-bold mb-1">Machine guns</h4><p class="text-xs text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">600 rounds per minute. One gun could mow down a battalion.</p></div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border border-ink-300 dark:border-ink-700 p-4 bg-white dark:bg-ink-900"><h4 class="font-serif font-bold mb-1">Poison gas</h4><p class="text-xs text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Chlorine and mustard gas. Burned lungs, blinded soldiers. Drifted with the wind.</p></div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border border-ink-300 dark:border-ink-700 p-4 bg-white dark:bg-ink-900"><h4 class="font-serif font-bold mb-1">Tanks</h4><p class="text-xs text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">First used by Britain at the Somme (1916). Slow, unreliable, but unstoppable when they worked.</p></div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border border-ink-300 dark:border-ink-700 p-4 bg-white dark:bg-ink-900"><h4 class="font-serif font-bold mb-1">Submarines (U-boats)</h4><p class="text-xs text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Germany sank merchant ships to starve Britain. Caused the Lusitania crisis (1915).</p></div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border border-ink-300 dark:border-ink-700 p-4 bg-white dark:bg-ink-900"><h4 class="font-serif font-bold mb-1">Airplanes</h4><p class="text-xs text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">First used for reconnaissance, then for dogfights and bombing. Aerial warfare was born.</p></div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border border-ink-300 dark:border-ink-700 p-4 bg-white dark:bg-ink-900"><h4 class="font-serif font-bold mb-1">Artillery</h4><p class="text-xs text-ink-700 dark:text-ink-300">Long-range guns lobbed shells miles. Caused the majority of WWI deaths and "shell shock."</p></div>
</div>

### Why the United States entered the war

For three years (1914-1917), the US held to its policy of **isolationism**. Then three things changed Wilson's mind.

<StepThrough
  title="Three reasons the US declared war (April 6, 1917)"
  steps={[
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:44px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">1</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">Unrestricted submarine warfare</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Germany announced it would sink any ship in war zones, including civilian. The 1915 sinking of the British liner <strong>Lusitania</strong> killed 128 Americans. Germany resumed unrestricted U-boat attacks in 1917.</p></div>`,
      caption: "Civilian deaths radicalized US public opinion."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:44px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">2</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">Strong economic ties to the Allies</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">US factories and banks had loaned billions to Britain and France. If they lost, the loans would default. The US economy was effectively backing the Allied side.</p></div>`,
      caption: "Money followed the British, then the troops did."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><div style="font-size:44px;font-weight:bold;color:#b91c1c;">3</div><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;margin:8px 0;">The Zimmerman Note (Jan 1917)</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Germany secretly proposed an alliance with Mexico against the US. British intelligence intercepted the telegram and shared it. Public outrage exploded. Wilson asked Congress for war.</p></div>`,
      caption: "The final straw."
    }
  ]}
  height={300}
/>

### Total war on the home front

<Callout type="insight" title="What 'Total War' means">
  Every part of society is mobilized for the war effort. Civilian factories build weapons. Food is rationed. Women take on industrial jobs. Propaganda saturates the press. The line between soldier and civilian disappears.
</Callout>

<div class="not-prose my-6 grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
  <div class="rounded-xl border-2 border-blue-700 bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/30 p-5">
    <h4 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-blue-900 dark:text-blue-100 mb-2">Allied home fronts</h4>
    <ul class="text-sm text-ink-800 dark:text-ink-200 list-disc list-inside space-y-1">
      <li>Britain rationed sugar, butter, meat by 1918.</li>
      <li>US Liberty Bonds raised billions.</li>
      <li>Women filled factory and munitions jobs in record numbers.</li>
      <li>Propaganda demonized "the Hun" (Germans).</li>
      <li>Press freedom restricted. Anti-war voices arrested.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>
  <div class="rounded-xl border-2 border-accent-700 bg-accent-50 dark:bg-accent-950/30 p-5">
    <h4 class="font-serif text-lg font-bold text-accent-900 dark:text-accent-100 mb-2">Central Powers home fronts</h4>
    <ul class="text-sm text-ink-800 dark:text-ink-200 list-disc list-inside space-y-1">
      <li>British naval blockade caused starvation in Germany by 1917.</li>
      <li>"Turnip winter" 1916-1917: Germans survived on turnips.</li>
      <li>Strikes erupted in Berlin and Vienna.</li>
      <li>Austria-Hungary's ethnic minorities began deserting.</li>
      <li>By 1918, civilian morale collapsed.</li>
    </ul>
  </div>
</div>

### Russia exits the war

The Eastern Front had been catastrophic for Russia. Over **5.5 million** Russian soldiers were dead, wounded, or captured by 1917. The food supply collapsed. The Tsar fell (March 1917). The new **Bolshevik** government, led by Lenin, signed the **Treaty of Brest-Litovsk** in March 1918, exiting the war and giving up huge territory: Poland, Ukraine, the Baltics, Finland.

<TremorCard label="Russian WWI casualties by 1917" value="~5.5 million" delta="Worst on either side" deltaType="decrease" helper="Dead, wounded, or captured. Drove the revolution that toppled the Tsar." />

For Germany, this was a brief gift: troops freed from the East could now flood west for a final offensive. But the gift came too late.

### The end: 1918

<StepThrough
  title="How WWI ended"
  steps={[
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:14px;">Spring 1918 · Germany's last gamble</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">With Russia out, Germany launched a massive Western offensive. Pushed within 50 miles of Paris. Then ran out of supplies and troops.</p></div>`,
      caption: "All in. Came up short."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:14px;">July 1918 · Second Battle of the Marne</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Two million fresh US troops break the German offensive. The Allies counter-attack and don't stop pushing.</p></div>`,
      caption: "American Expeditionary Force tips the balance."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:14px;">Fall 1918 · Central Powers collapse</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">Bulgaria surrenders Sept 29. Ottomans surrender Oct 30. Austria-Hungary surrenders Nov 3. Kaiser Wilhelm II abdicates Nov 9. Germany is alone.</p></div>`,
      caption: "Dominoes fall the other way."
    },
    {
      html: `<div style="padding:20px;max-width:560px;text-align:center;"><h3 style="font-family:serif;font-size:20px;color:#b91c1c;margin-bottom:14px;">Nov 11, 1918 · Armistice</h3><p style="font-size:14px;color:#52525b;line-height:1.5;">11 a.m. on the 11th day of the 11th month. Germany signs a ceasefire in a railway car in France. WWI is over.</p></div>`,
      caption: "20 million dead. 21 million wounded. 4 empires destroyed."
    }
  ]}
  height={300}
/>
